They\nare the rules `tools/check_plan.py` applies.\n\n## Orders\n\nEach file in `orders/` is one morning. It lists the bakery (`depot`), the\ncustomers to deliver to, and the vehicles available (`fleet`). Positions are\ninteger grid coordinates `x`, `y` in units of 100 m; times are seconds after\nmidnight. The bakery opens at `open` and closes at `close`.\n\nA customer has an integer `id`, a position, a `demand` in crates, a delivery\nwindow `ready`..`due`, and a `service` time in seconds for unloading. Its\n`kind` (cafe, grocer, hotel, market) is for information only and changes no\nrule.\n\nA fleet entry is one kind of vehicle, named by its `type`: `count` vehicles of it are available,\neach carrying up to `capacity` crates. It travels at `pace` seconds per unit of\ndistance, costs `fixed` for being used at all and `per_unit` for every unit of\ndistance it drives, may be out for at most `shift` seconds from leaving the\nbakery to getting back, and, where `range` is not null, may drive at most\n`range` units of distance in the morning. Costs are in cents.\n\n## Distance and time\n\nThe distance between two points is the Euclidean distance between them,\nrounded up to a whole unit. A vehicle takes `distance * pace` seconds to drive\nit.\n\n## A route\n\nA route is one vehicle's morning: it leaves the bakery at its departure time,\nvisits its customers in order, and comes back. Every route visits at least one\ncustomer, and each vehicle runs at most one route.\n\n- The departure is a whole number of seconds, not before the bakery opens.\n- At each customer the vehicle unloads from the later of its arrival and the\n  customer's `ready` time, and that must not be after `due`. Unloading takes the\n  customer's `service` time; the vehicle leaves as soon as it is done.\n- The vehicle must be back at the bakery no later than it closes, and no more\n  than `shift` seconds after its departure.\n- The crates on the route (the sum of its customers' demands) must fit the\n  vehicle's `capacity`, and the distance driven must be within its `range`\n  where it has one. The distance driven is the sum of the rounded legs, from\n  the bakery through each customer in turn and back to the bakery.\n\n## A plan\n\nA plan delivers to every customer of the morning exactly once, uses no more\nvehicles of a kind than the fleet has, and costs the sum over its routes of the\nvehicle's `fixed` cost plus `per_unit` times the route's distance.\n\nIt is written as JSON:\n\n    {\"routes\": [{\"type\": \"van\", \"depart\": 18000, \"stops\": [12, 4, 31]}, ...]}\n\nwhere `type` names a fleet entry, `depart` is the departure time as a JSON\ninteger (`18000`, not `18000.0`) and `stops` lists customer ids, as the\nintegers in the orders file, in visiting order. Other keys are ignored.\n\n--- targets ---\n{\n \"harbour-lanes\": 77867,\n \"old-town\": 147362,\n \"riverside\": 201921,\n \"north-ring\": 288705\n}\n--- planner ---\n\"\"\"Current dispatch planner: nearest-next greedy, vans first.\n\nUsage: python3 planner/plan_morning.py orders/<morning>.json plans/<morning>.json\n\nBuilds one route at a time. A route leaves when the bakery opens and keeps\ndriving to the nearest customer it can still reach inside that customer's window\nwith room on board; when nothing fits, it goes home and the next vehicle starts.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), \"..\", \"tools\"))\nfrom check_plan import distance, evaluate  # noqa: E402\n\nORDER = (\"van\", \"truck\", \"bike\")\n\n\ndef plan(orders):\n    depot = orders[\"depot\"]\n    left = {c[\"id\"]: c for c in orders[\"customers\"]}\n    fleet = sorted(orders[\"fleet\"], key=lambda f: ORDER.index(f[\"type\"]) if f[\"type\"] in ORDER else 99)\n    routes = []\n    for kind in fleet:\n        for _ in range(kind[\"count\"]):\n            if not left:\n                break\n            clock, here, crates, driven, stops = depot[\"open\"], depot, 0, 0, []\n            while True:\n                best = None\n                for c in c[\"demand\"] > kind[\"capacity\"]:\n                        continue\n                    back = start + c[\"service\"] + distance(c, depot) * kind[\"pace\"]\n                    if back > depot[\"close\"] or back - depot[\"open\"] > kind[\"shift\"]:\n                        continue\n                    total = driven + leg + distance(c, depot)\n                    if kind[\"range\"] is not None and total > kind[\"range\"]:\n                        continue\n                    if best is None or leg < best[0]:\n                        best = (leg, c, start)\n                if best is None:\n                    break\n                leg, c, start = best\n                stops.append(c[\"id\"])\n                clock, here, crates, driven = start + c[\"service\"], c, crates + c[\"demand\"], driven + leg\n                del left[c[\"id\"]]\n            if stops:\n                routes.append({\"type\": kind[\"type\"], \"depart\": depot[\"open\"], \"stops\": stops})\n plans/<morning>.json\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport math\nimport sys\n\n\nclass PlanError(ValueError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef distance(a, b):\n    sq = (a[\"x\"] - b[\"x\"]) ** 2 + (a[\"y\"] - b[\"y\"]) ** 2\n    d = math.isqrt(sq)\n    return d if d * d == sq else d + 1\n\n\ndef evaluate(orders, plan):\n    depot = orders[\"depot\"]\n    customers = {c[\"id\"]: c for c in orders[\"customers\"]}\n    fleet = {f[\"type\"]: f for f in orders[\"fleet\"]}\n    used = {t: 0 for t in fleet}\n    served = set()\n    total = 0\n    if not isinstance(plan, dict) or not isinstance(plan.get(\"routes\"), PlanError(f\"{where}: departure must be a whole second, not before opening\")\n        used[route[\"type\"]] += 1\n        clock, here, crates, driven = depart, depot, 0, 0\n        for cid in {"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_15","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"cat > /tmp/solve_dispatch.py <<'PY'\nimport json, math, random, sys, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT=Path('/app')\n\ndef dist(a,b):\n    q=(a['x']-b['x'])**2+(a['y']-b['y'])**2\n    d=math.isqrt(q)\n    return d if d*d==q else d+1\n\nclass Solver:\n def __init__(self,name,seed=1):\n  self.name=name; self.data=json.load(open(ROOT/'orders'/f'{name}.json')); self.rng=random.Random(seed)\n  self.cs=self.data['customers']; self.n=len(self.cs); self.c={x['id']:x for x in self.cs}; self.dep=self.data['depot']\n  self.types={x['type']:x for x in self.data['fleet']}\n  self.D={}\n  pts=[self.dep]+self.cs\n  for a in pts:\n   for b in pts:self.D[(a.get('id',0),b.get('id',0))]=dist(a,b)\n  total=sum(x['demand'] for x in self.cs)\n  bike=next(x for x in self.data['fleet'] if x['type']=='bike'); van=next(x for x in self.data['fleet'] if x['type']=='van'); truck=next(x for x in self.data['fleet'] if x['type']=='truck')\n  kt=max(0,math.ceil((total-bike['count']*bike['capacity']-van['count']*van['capacity'])/truck['capacity']))\n  self.route_types=['bike']*bike['count']+['van']*van['count']+['truck']*kt\n  self.eval_cache={}\n\n def route_eval(self,t,seq):\n  if not seq:return (True,0, self.dep['open'])\n  key=(t,tuple(seq)); z=self.eval_cache.get(key)\n  if z is not None:return z\n  k=self.types[t]\n  demand=sum(self.c[i]['demand'] for i in seq)\n  if demand>k['capacity']:\n   z=(False,0,0);self.eval_cache[key]=z;return z\n  driven=self.D[(0,seq[0])]+sum(self.D[(a,b)] for a,b in zip(seq,seq[1:]))+self.D[(seq[-1],0)]\n  if k['range'] is not None and driven>k['range']:\n   z=(False,0,0);self.eval_cache[key]=z;return z\n  latest=self.dep['close']-self.D[(seq[-1],0)]*k['pace']-self.c[seq[-1]]['service']\n  latest=min(latest,self.c[seq[-1]]['due'])\n  for i in range(len(seq)-2,-1,-1):\n   a,b=seq[i],seq[i+1]\n   latest=min(self.c[a]['due'],latest-self.D[(a,b)]*k['pace']-self.c[a]['service'])\n  depart=latest-self.D[(0,seq[0])]*k['pace']\n  if depart<self.dep['open']:\n   depart=self.dep['open']\n  clock=depart; prev=0\n  for cid in seq:\n   x=self.c[cid]; clock=max(clock+self.D[(prev,cid)]*k['pace'],x['ready'])\n   if clock>x['due']:\n    z=(False,0,0);self.eval_cache[key]=z;return z\n   clock+=x['service'];prev=cid\n  back=clock+self.D[(prev,0)]*k['pace']\n  if back>self.dep['close'] or back-depart>k['shift']:\n   z=(False,0,0);self.eval_cache[key]=z;return z\n  cost=k['fixed']+k['per_unit']*driven\n  z=(True,cost,depart);self.eval_cache[key]=z;return z\n\n def cost(self,routes):\n  return sum(self.route_eval(t,s)[1] for t,s in zip(self.route_types,routes) if s)\n\n def insertion_options(self,routes,cid,noise=0.0,limit=None):\n  opts=[]\n  for ri,(t,s) in enumerate(zip(self.route_types,routes)):\n   old=self.route_eval(t,s)[1] if s else 0\n   for p in range(len(s)+1):\n    ns=s[:p]+[cid]+s[p:]\n    ok,nc,_=self.route_eval(t,ns)\n    if ok:\n     delta=nc-old\n     score=delta*(1+self.rng.uniform(-noise,noise))\n     opts.append((score,delta,ri,p))\n  opts.sort()\n  return opts if limit is None else opts[:limit]\n\n def construct(self, randomize=.12):\n  routes=[[] for _ in self.route_types]\n  un=set(x['id'] for x in self.cs)\n  # Put customers exceeding van capacity into trucks first (none currently expected).\n  while un:\n   chosen=None\n   sample=list(un)\n   if len(sample)>80: sample=self.rng.sample(sample,80)\n   for cid in sample:\n    opts=self.insertion_options(routes,cid,randomize,3)\n    if not opts: continue\n    regret=(opts[1][0]-opts[0][0] if len(opts)>1 else rules ---\\n# Morning dispatch rules\\n\\nThese rules decide whether a delivery plan can be run and what it costs. They\\nare the rules `tools/check_plan.py` applies.\\n\\n## Orders\\n\\nEach file in `orders/` is one morning. It lists the bakery (`depot`), the\\ncustomers to deliver to, and the vehicles available (`fleet`). Positions are\\ninteger grid coordinates `x`, `y` in units of 100 m; times are seconds after\\nmidnight. The bakery opens at `open` and closes at `close`.\\n\\nA customer has an integer `id`, a position, a `demand` in crates, a delivery\\nwindow `ready`..`due`, and a `service` time in seconds for unloading. Its\\n`kind` (cafe, grocer, hotel, market) is for information only and changes no\\nrule.\\n\\nA fleet entry is one kind of vehicle, named by its `type`: `count` vehicles of it are available,\\neach carrying up to `capacity` crates. It travels at `pace` seconds per unit of\\ndistance, costs `fixed` for being used at all and `per_unit` for every unit of\\ndistance it drives, may be out for at most `shift` seconds from leaving the\\nbakery to getting back, and, where `range` is not null, may drive at most\\n`range` units of distance in the morning. Costs are in cents.\\n\\n## Distance and time\\n\\nThe distance between two points is the Euclidean distance between them,\\nrounded up to a whole unit. A vehicle takes `distance * pace` seconds to drive\\nit.\\n\\n## A route\\n\\nA route is one vehicle's morning: it leaves the bakery at its departure time,\\nvisits its customers in order, and comes back. Every route visits at least one\\ncustomer, and each vehicle runs at most one route.\\n\\n- The departure is a whole number of seconds, not before the bakery opens.\\n- At each customer the vehicle unloads from the later of its arrival and the\\n  customer's `ready` time, and that must not be after `due`. Unloading takes the\\n  customer's `service` time; the vehicle leaves as soon as it is done.\\n- The vehicle must be back at the bakery no later than it closes, and no more\\n  than `shift` seconds after its departure.\\n- The crates on the route (the sum of its customers' demands) must fit the\\n  vehicle's `capacity`, and the distance driven must be within its `range`\\n  where it has one. The distance driven is the sum of the rounded legs, from\\n  the bakery through each customer in turn and back to the bakery.\\n\\n## A plan\\n\\nA plan delivers to every customer of the morning exactly once, uses no more\\nvehicles of a kind than the fleet has, and costs the sum over its routes of the\\nvehicle's `fixed` cost plus `per_unit` times the route's distance.\\n\\nIt is written as JSON:\\n\\n    {\\\"routes\\\": [{\\\"type\\\": \\\"van\\\", \\\"depart\\\": 18000, \\\"stops\\\": [12, 4, 31]}, ...]}\\n\\nwhere `type` names a fleet entry, `depart` is the departure time as a JSON\\ninteger (`18000`, not `18000.0`) and `stops` lists customer ids, as the\\nintegers in the orders file, in visiting order. Other keys are ignored.\\n\\n--- targets ---\\n{\\n \\\"harbour-lanes\\\": 77867,\\n \\\"old-town\\\": 147362,\\n \\\"riverside\\\": 201921,\\n \\\"north-ring\\\": 288705\\n}\\n--- planner ---\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Current dispatch planner: nearest-next greedy, vans first.\\n\\nUsage: python3 planner/plan_morning.py orders/<morning>.json plans/<morning>.json\\n\\nBuilds one route at a time. A route leaves when the bakery opens and keeps\\ndriving to the nearest customer it can still reach inside that customer's window\\nwith room on board; when nothing fits, it goes home and the next vehicle starts.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), \\\"..\\\", \\\"tools\\\"))\\nfrom check_plan rules ---\\n# Morning dispatch rules\\n\\nThese rules decide whether a delivery plan can be run and what it costs. They\\nare the rules `tools/check_plan.py` applies.\\n\\n## Orders\\n\\nEach file in `orders/` is one morning. It lists the bakery (`depot`), the\\ncustomers to deliver to, and the vehicles available (`fleet`). Positions are\\ninteger grid coordinates `x`, `y` in units of 100 m; times are seconds after\\nmidnight. The bakery opens at `open` and closes at `close`.\\n\\nA customer has an integer `id`, a position, a `demand` in crates, a delivery\\nwindow `ready`..`due`, and a `service` time in seconds for unloading. Its\\n`kind` (cafe, grocer, hotel, market) is for information only and changes no\\nrule.\\n\\nA fleet entry is one kind of vehicle, named by its `type`: `count` vehicles of it are available,\\neach carrying up to `capacity` crates. It travels at `pace` seconds per unit of\\ndistance, costs `fixed` for being used at all and `per_unit` for every unit of\\ndistance it drives, may be out for at most `shift` seconds from leaving the\\nbakery to getting back, and, where `range` is not null, may drive at most\\n`range` units of distance in the morning. Costs are in cents.\\n\\n## Distance and time\\n\\nThe distance between two points is the Euclidean distance between them,\\nrounded up to a whole unit. A vehicle takes `distance * pace` seconds to drive\\nit.\\n\\n## A route\\n\\nA route is one vehicle's morning: it leaves the bakery at its departure time,\\nvisits its customers in order, and comes back. Every route visits at least one\\ncustomer, and each vehicle runs at most one route.\\n\\n- The departure is a whole number of seconds, not before the bakery opens.\\n- At each customer the vehicle unloads from the later of its arrival and the\\n  customer's `ready` time, and that must not be after `due`. Unloading takes the\\n  customer's `service` time; the vehicle leaves as soon as it is done.\\n- The vehicle must be back at the bakery no later than it closes, and no more\\n  than `shift` seconds after its departure.\\n- The crates on the route (the sum of its customers' demands) must fit the\\n  vehicle's `capacity`, and the distance driven must be within its `range`\\n  where it has one. The distance driven is the sum of the rounded legs, from\\n  the bakery through each customer in turn and back to the bakery.\\n\\n## A plan\\n\\nA plan delivers to every customer of the morning exactly once, uses no more\\nvehicles of a kind than the fleet has, and costs the sum over its routes of the\\nvehicle's `fixed` cost plus `per_unit` times the route's distance.\\n\\nIt is written as JSON:\\n\\n    {\\\"routes\\\": [{\\\"type\\\": \\\"van\\\", \\\"depart\\\": 18000, \\\"stops\\\": [12, 4, 31]}, ...]}\\n\\nwhere `type` names a fleet entry, `depart` is the departure time as a JSON\\ninteger (`18000`, not `18000.0`) and `stops` lists customer ids, as the\\nintegers in the orders file, in visiting order. Other keys are ignored.\\n\\n--- targets ---\\n{\\n \\\"harbour-lanes\\\": 77867,\\n \\\"old-town\\\": 147362,\\n \\\"riverside\\\": 201921,\\n \\\"north-ring\\\": 288705\\n}\\n--- planner ---\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Current dispatch planner: nearest-next greedy, vans first.\\n\\nUsage: python3 planner/plan_morning.py orders/<morning>.json plans/<morning>.json\\n\\nBuilds one route at a time. score=delta*(1+self.rng.uniform(-noise,noise))\\n     opts.append((score,delta,ri,p))\\n  opts.sort()\\n  return opts if limit is None else opts[:limit]\\n\\n def construct(self, randomize=.12):\\n best[0]<base:routes[ri]=best[1];changed=True\\n  return routes\\n\\n def local_relocate(self,routes,first=True,max_pass=4):\\n  for _ in range(max_pass):\\n   bestmove=None\\n {"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_63","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"cat > /tmp/sweep.py <<'PY'\nimport sys,json,math,random,time\nsys.path.insert(0,'/tmp');from solve_dispatch import Solver\nname,out,secs,seed=sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2],float(sys.argv[3]),int(sys.argv[4]); rng=random.Random(seed);s=Solver(name,seed)\n# route counts inferred by low-fixed feasible patterns\nmix={'harbour-lanes':(4,7,1),'old-town':(9,12,3),'riverside':(12,20,3),'north-ring':(15,27,6)}[name]\ns.route_types=['bike']*mix[0]+['van']*mix[1]+['truck']*mix[2]\nend=time.time()+secs;best=None;bc=10**18;it=0\nwhile time.time()<end:\n it+=1;routes=[[] for _ in s.route_types]\n rot=rng.random()*2*math.pi\n # angle primary with substantial randomized band/timing variants\n vals=[]\n for c in s.cs:\n  ang=(math.atan2(c['y'],c['x'])-rot)%(2*math.pi)\n  if it%4==0:key=ang+rng.gauss(0,.18)\n  elif it%4==1:key=ang + (c['ready']-25000)/80000 + rng.gauss(0,.08)\n  elif it%4==2:key=ang + math.hypot(c['x'],c['y'])/1200 + rng.gauss(0,.1)\n  else:key=ang+rng.gauss(0,.35)\n  vals.append((key,c['id']))\n vals.sort()\n if rng.random()<.5:vals.reverse()\n # Routes are opened and filled locally; random tie choices diversify.\n fail=False\n for _,cid in vals:\n  op=s.insertion_options(routes,cid,.08,8)\n  if not op:fail=True;break\n  # bias toward routes already used and nearby, but exact delta dominates\n  ranked=[]\n  for score,delta,ri,p in op:\n   empty=not routes[ri]\n   ranked.append((score + (500 if empty else 0)+rng.random()*80,ri,p))\n  ranked.sort(); _,ri,p=ranked[0 if rng.random()<.9 else rng.randrange(min(3,len(ranked)))]\n  routes[ri].insert(p,cid)\n if fail:continue\n c=s.cost(routes)\n if c<bc:\n  best,bc=[x[:] for x in routes],c;print('construct',it,bc,flush=True)\n  x=s.improve_order([q[:] for q in best]);x=s.local_relocate(x,False,4);cc=s.cost(x)\n  if cc<bc:best,bc=x,cc;print('local',bc,flush=True)\nif best is None:raise SystemExit('none')\nbest=s.improve_order(best);best=s.local_relocate(best,False,12);best=s.local_swap(best,6);bc=s.cost(best)\np={'routes':[]}\nfor t,q in zip(s.route_types,best):\n if q:\n  ok,c,d=s.route_eval(t,q);p['routes'].append({'type':t,'depart':d,'stops':q})\njson.dump(p,open(out,'w'),indent=1);print('FINAL',bc)\nPY\npython3 /tmp/sweep.py harbour-lanes /tmp/hsweep.json 120 201\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_77","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"cat > /tmp/route_anneal.py <<'PY'\nimport sys,json,time,math,random\nsys.path.insert(0,'/tmp');from solve_dispatch import Solver\nname,inp,out,secs,seed=sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2],sys.argv[3],float(sys.argv[4]),int(sys.argv[5]);s=Solver(name,seed);raw=json.load(open(inp));order={'bike':0,'van':1,'truck':2};s.route_types=sorted([r['type'] for r in raw['routes']],key=lambda t:order[t]);routes=s.routes_from_plan(inp);rng=s.rng\nprint('start',s.cost(routes),flush=True)\nend=time.time()+secs\nwhile time.time()<end:\n ri=rng.randrange(len(routes));q=routes[ri]\n if len(q)<3:continue\n t=s.route_types[ri];old=s.route_eval(t,q)[1]\n nq=q[:];mode=rng.random()\n if mode<.35:\n  i,j=rng.sample(range(len(q)),2);nq[i],nq[j]=nq[j],nq[i]\n elif mode<.7:\n  i,j=sorted(rng.sample(range(len(q)+1),2));nq[i:j]=reversed(nq[i:j])\n else:\n  i=rng.randrange(len(q));x=nq.pop(i);j=rng.randrange(len(nq)+1);nq.insert(j,x)\n ok,nc,_=s.route_eval(t,nq)\n if not ok:continue\n rem=(end-time.time())/secs;temp=300*rem+2\n if nc<old or rng.random()<math.exp((old-nc)/temp):routes[ri]=nq\n# local and emit\nroutes=s.improve_order(routes);print('end',s.cost(routes),flush=True)\np={'routes':[]}\nfor t,q in zip(s.route_types,routes):\n if q:\n  ok,c,d=s.route_eval(t,q);p['routes'].append({'type':t,'depart':d,'stops':q})\njson.dump(p,open(out,'w'),indent=1)\nPY\npython3 /tmp/route_anneal.py harbour-lanes /tmp/harbour4.json /tmp/hann.json 180 601\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_85","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"cat > /tmp/crossover.py <<'PY'\nimport sys,json,random,time,glob\nsys.path.insert(0,'/tmp');from solve_dispatch import Solver\nname,out,secs,seed,*files=sys.argv[1:];secs=float(secs);s=Solver(name,int(seed));rng=s.rng\nparents=[]\nfor f in files:\n try:r=s.routes_from_plan(f)\n except:continue\n if sum(len(x) for x in r)==s.n:parents.append(r)\nbest=min(parents,key=s.cost);bc=s.cost(best);print('start',bc,len(parents),flush=True);end=time.time()+secs;it=0\nwhile time.time()<end:\n it+=1;a,b=rng.sample(parents,2); child=[[] for _ in s.route_types];used=set()\n inds=list(range(len(child)));rng.shuffle(inds)\n # inherit 25-65% complete routes from either parent, collision-free\n for i in inds[:rng.randint(3,8)]:\n  src=a if rng.random()<.5 else b\n  q=src[i]\n  if q and not any(x in used for x in q):child[i]=q[:];used.update(q)\n rem=[c['id'] for c in s.cs if c['id'] not in used]\n child=s.repair(child,rem,.35,True)\n if child is None:continue\n c=s.cost(child)\n child=s.local_relocate(child,True,4);c=s.cost(child)\n if c<bc:\n  best,bc=[x[:] for x in child],c;parents.append(best);parents=sorted(parents,key=s.cost)[:12];print('best',bc,it,flush=True)\nbest=s.improve_order(best);best=s.local_relocate(best,False,10);best=s.local_swap(best,8);bc=s.cost(best)\np={'routes':[]}\nfor t,q in zip(s.route_types,best):\n if q:\n  ok,c,d=s.route_eval(t,q);p['routes'].append({'type':t,'depart':d,'stops':q})\njson.dump(p,open(out,'w'),indent=1);print('FINAL',bc)\nPY\npython3 /tmp/crossover.py harbour-lanes /tmp/hcross.json 300 901 /tmp/harbour4.json /tmp/hpop801.json /tmp/hpop803.json /tmp/hpop804.json /tmp/hpop805.json /tmp/hpop806.json\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}
